[user message]
For each integer \(n\ge2\), let \(F_n\) be the free group on \(n\) generators. For any group \(G\), define its lower central series by \(\gamma_1(G)=G\) and \(\gamma_{r+1}(G)=[\gamma_r(G),G]\), where \([H,K]\) is the subgroup generated by the commutators \([h,k]=hkh^{-1}k^{-1}\) with \(h\in H\) and \(k\in K\). For every integer \(r\ge1\), define
\[
\mathcal A_r(n)=\ker\!\left(\operatorname{Aut}(F_n)\longrightarrow\operatorname{Aut}\bigl(F_n/\gamma_{r+1}(F_n)\bigr)\right),
\qquad \operatorname{IA}_n=\mathcal A_1(n).
\]
Determine, by explicit conditions on \(n\) and \(c\), the complete set
\[
\mathcal D=\left\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}:\ 
\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq
\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)
\right\}.
\]

The answer is \mathcal D=\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}: n\ge3,\ c\ge6\}. This was proved recently, so it is not an open problem: your team's task is to prove it, completely, and to write Python that really computes or checks every step that can be checked. Printing what you believe is not evidence. Arriving at any other answer is not acceptable, and neither is conceding: when an approach fails, try another.

Proving it takes two halves: for every \(n\ge3\) and \(c\ge6\), \(\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)\), and for every \(n\ge2\) and \(c\ge1\) with \(n=2\) or \(c\le5\), \(\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)=\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)\). Split the work, and the pieces of each part, between you on Slack.

Stop only when the team's proof is complete. Before you stop, post on Slack what you proved, and what is left.

```
#14 agent_4:
Rank2 proof complete modulo classical Nielsen/Magnus–Witt facts. Magnus–Witt identifies grγ(F_n) with free Lie subring L⊂Z⟨X1,…,Xn⟩. Its positive-degree center is zero: a homogeneous associative polynomial commuting with X1 must be a multiple of X1^d (rotate leading X1's in each word's coefficient until reaching a word starting with another letter, which must have coefficient zero); commuting with X2 then forces zero. Thus if [g,F_n]⊂γ_{c+2} but g∉γ_{c+1}, take r≤c with g∈γr\γr+1; its nonzero degree-r leading class commutes with all Xi, contradiction. Hence Z(F_n/γ_{c+2})=γ_{c+1}/γ_{c+2}. Since IA2=InnF2≅F2 and γc Inn=Inn γc, γc(IA2)∩A_{c+1}=Inn γ_{c+1}=γ_{c+1}(IA2), every c. Exact free-word certificates are workspace/rank2_certificate.py.
```

content:
```
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('workspace/agent3_magnus.py')
p.write_text('''"""Exact reduced-word and truncated integral Magnus computations.
No numerical linear algebra or inferred identities.  Words use signed 1-based letters.
"""
from collections import defaultdict

def reduce_word(w):
    out=[]
    for a in w:
        if out and out[-1]==-a: out.pop()
        else: out.append(a)
    return tuple(out)

def inverse(w): return tuple(-a for a in reversed(w))
def product(*ws): return reduce_word(a for w in ws for a in w)
def substitute(w, images):
    return product(*(images[a-1] if a>0 else inverse(images[-a-1]) for a in w))
def compose(f,g): return tuple(substitute(w,f) for w in g)
def auto_commutator(f,fi,g,gi): return compose(compose(compose(f,g),fi),gi)
def word_commutator(u,v): return product(u,v,inverse(u),inverse(v))
def identity(n): return tuple((i,) for i in range(1,n+1))
def multiply(a,b,degree):
    out=defaultdict(int)
    for u,x in a.items():
        for v,y in b.items():
            if len(u)+len(v)<=degree: out[u+v]+=x*y
    return {w:c for w,c in out.items() if c}
def magnus(w,degree):
    out={():1}
    for a in w:
        i=abs(a)
        factor={():1,(i,):1} if a>0 else {(i,)*k:(-1)**k for k in range(degree+1)}
        out=multiply(out,factor,degree)
    return out

def johnson_terms(images,degree):
    """Return Magnus expansions of f(x_i)x_i^-1, less constant 1, through degree."""
    out=[]
    for i,w in enumerate(images,1):
        q=magnus(product(w,(-i,)),degree)
        q.pop((),None)
        out.append(q)
    return tuple(out)
def is_andreadakis(images,r):
    """Exact test f in A_r, assuming images define an automorphism.
    Magnus dimension theorem over Z: w in gamma_(r+1) iff terms <=r vanish.
    """
    return not any(johnson_terms(images,r))
def K(n,i,j):
    f=list(identity(n)); f[i-1]=(j,i,-j)
    fi=list(identity(n)); fi[i-1]=(-j,i,j)
    return tuple(f),tuple(fi)
def C(n,i,j,k):
    f=list(identity(n)); h=word_commutator((j,),(k,)); f[i-1]=product((i,),h)
    fi=list(identity(n)); fi[i-1]=product((i,),inverse(h))
    return tuple(f),tuple(fi)

def self_test():
    for n in range(2,6):
        gens=[]
        for i in range(1,n+1):
            for j in range(1,n+1):
                if i!=j: gens.append(K(n,i,j))
            for j in range(1,n+1):
                for k in range(j+1,n+1):
                    if i not in (j,k): gens.append(C(n,i,j,k))
        for f,fi in gens:
            assert compose(f,fi)==compose(fi,f)==identity(n)
            assert is_andreadakis(f,1) and not is_andreadakis(f,2)
        # Every pair is checked, not sampled.
        for f,fi in gens:
            for g,gi in gens:
                h=auto_commutator(f,fi,g,gi)
                assert is_andreadakis(h,2)
        print('n=',n,'generators=',len(gens),'pair-commutators checked=',len(gens)**2)
    # A genuine nonzero degree-two Johnson image.
    f,fi=K(3,1,2); g,gi=K(3,2,3)
    h=auto_commutator(f,fi,g,gi)
    assert not is_andreadakis(h,3)
    print('nonzero degree-two leading terms:',johnson_terms(h,3))
if __name__=='__main__': self_test()
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
import agent3_magnus
agent3_magnus.self_test()
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent3_degree2_lattice_lemma.md').write_text(r'''# A finite integral certificate criterion for degree-two injectivity

Let G be a group, let V=G/[G,G] be free abelian of finite rank, and let
beta: Lambda^2 V -> gamma_2(G)/gamma_3(G)
be the canonical surjection, beta(u wedge v)=[u,v].
Let J: gamma_2(G)/gamma_3(G) -> W be a homomorphism to a free abelian group.
Suppose an integer matrix B represents J beta.

For each pair of commuting elements a,b in G, the column
[a]_ab wedge [b]_ab belongs to ker beta. More generally, if theta is any
automorphism of G, then theta_ab([a]_ab) wedge theta_ab([b]_ab) belongs
to ker beta, because theta(a),theta(b) still commute.

Form the integer matrix R whose columns are any such relations. If
(1) B R=0,
(2) rank_Q R = dim_Q ker B,
(3) every nonzero Smith invariant of R is 1,
then J is injective.

Proof: let N be the number of rows of R. Condition (3) says that
Z^N/im R is torsion-free, i.e. im R is saturated. Conditions (1),(2)
then imply im R=ker B: for x in ker B, equality of rational spans gives
a nonzero integer m with mx in im R; saturation gives x in im R.
Every R column is killed by beta, while ker beta is contained in ker B,
so ker beta=ker B. Therefore the induced J is injective. No presentation
of G, finiteness assumption on its relation set, or torsion-freeness
assumption on gamma_2(G)/gamma_3(G) is used.

For G=IA_n, V=H^* tensor Lambda^2 H by the Magnus-generator argument.
Take W=Hom(H,L_3(H)), or its canonical embedding in
Hom(H,H^{tensor 3}). Then B is the degree-two derivation bracket map.
The standard Johnson calculation identifies J with the restriction of
tau_2 to gamma_2(G). Therefore the three conditions prove
 gamma_2(IA_n) intersect A_3(n) = gamma_3(IA_n).
Combining with A_2(n)=gamma_2(IA_n) gives A_3(n)=gamma_3(IA_n).

CAUTION: rational ranks alone do not suffice: condition (3) is essential.
''')
print('wrote workspace/agent3_degree2_lattice_lemma.md')
```

```
"""An exact integral proof certificate for degree-2 injectivity for IA_3.
Dependencies: Magnus generation and the degree-one Johnson homomorphism.
Relations used are genuine commuting pairs and their Nielsen conjugates.
"""
import sys
from itertools import combinations,product,permutations
from sympy import Matrix,ZZ
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form
from degree1_certificate import generators,expansion

def red(w):
 s=[]
 for x in w:
  if s and s[-1]==-x:s.pop()
  else:s.append(x)
 return tuple(s)
def inv(w):return tuple(-x for x in reversed(w))
def compose(f,g):
 return tuple(red(y for x in w for y in (f[x-1] if x>0 else inv(f[-x-1]))) for w in g)
def add(a,b,scale=1):
 a=a.copy()
 for w,v in b.items():a[w]=a.get(w,0)+scale*v
 return {w:v for w,v in a.items() if v}
def deriv(d,p):
 o={}
 for w,v in p.items():
  for t,i in enumerate(w):
   for u,x in d[i].items():
    key=w[:t]+u+w[t+1:];o[key]=o.get(key,0)+v*x
 return {w:v for w,v in o.items() if v}
def certificate(n=3):
 ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));gs=[];ds=[]
 for _,i,w in generators(n):
  g=list(ident);g[i]=w;gs.append(tuple(g))
  p=add(expansion(w+(-i-1,)),{():1},-1)
  d=[{} for _ in range(n)];d[i]=p;ds.append(d)
 m=len(gs);pairs=list(combinations(range(m),2));bp={p:i for i,p in enumerate(pairs)}
 cols=[]
 for i,j in pairs:
  D=[add(deriv(ds[i],ds[j][k]),deriv(ds[j],ds[i][k]),-1) for k in range(n)]
  cols.append([D[k].get(w,0) for k in range(n) for w in product(range(n),repeat=3)])
 B=Matrix(cols).T
 rankB=B.rank()
 print('n',n,'m',m,'bracket rank',rankB,'null',len(pairs)-rankB,flush=True)
 cand=[]
 for i,g in enumerate(gs):cand.append((g,{i:1},('g',i)))
 for i,j in combinations(range(m),2):
  cand.append((compose(gs[i],gs[j]),{i:1,j:1},('p',i,j)))
  cand.append((compose(gs[j],gs[i]),{i:1,j:1},('p',j,i)))
 R=[];seen=set();cert=[]
 for (a,u,namea),(b,v,nameb) in combinations(cand,2):
  if compose(a,b)!=compose(b,a):continue
  r=[0]*len(pairs)
  for i,y in u.items():
   for j,x in v.items():
    if i<j:r[bp[i,j]]+=x*y
    if i>j:r[bp[j,i]]-=x*y
  t=tuple(r)
  if any(r) and t not in seen:
   seen.add(t);R.append(r);cert.append((namea,nameb))
 M=Matrix(R).T
 print('relations',len(R),'rank',M.rank(),flush=True)
 assert B*M==Matrix.zeros(B.rows,M.cols)
 keys=[];signs=[]
 for D in ds:
  k=next(k for k,p in enumerate(D) if p)
  w=next(w for w in D[k] if w[0]<w[1])
  keys.append((k,w));signs.append(D[k][w])
 actions=[]
 for ii,jj in permutations(range(n),2):
  t=list(ident);ti=list(ident)
  t[ii]=(ii+1,jj+1);ti[ii]=(ii+1,-jj-1)
  assert compose(t,ti)==ident and compose(ti,t)==ident
  C=[]
  for g in gs:
   h=compose(t,compose(g,ti))
   hd=[add(expansion(h[k]+(-k-1,)),{():1},-1) for k in range(n)]
   C.append([hd[k].get(w,0)*sgn for (k,w),sgn in zip(keys,signs)])
  A=Matrix(C).T
  W=Matrix([[A[i,p]*A[j,q]-A[i,q]*A[j,p] for p,q in pairs] for i,j in pairs])
  actions.append(W)
 M=Matrix.hstack(M,*[W*M for W in actions])
 rankM=M.rank()
 print('GL-closure relations',M.cols,'rank',rankM,flush=True)
 assert B*M==Matrix.zeros(B.rows,M.cols)
 S=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ)
 diag=[abs(S[i,i]) for i in range(min(S.shape)) if S[i,i]]
 print('SNF',diag,flush=True)
 assert rankM==len(pairs)-rankB
 assert all(x==1 for x in diag)
 print('PASS: actual group relations equal the full integral bracket kernel')
 return B,M,cert

if __name__=='__main__':certificate(int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 3)
```

```
"""Exact degree-one Johnson-map check for Magnus generators.
No theorem about degrees >=2 is asserted by this computation.
"""
from itertools import combinations

def multiply(a,b,degree=2):
    c={}
    for u,x in a.items():
        for v,y in b.items():
            if len(u)+len(v)<=degree:
                w=u+v;c[w]=c.get(w,0)+x*y
    return {w:x for w,x in c.items() if x}

def expansion(word):
    out={():1}
    for s in word:
        i=abs(s)-1
        factor={():1,(i,):1} if s>0 else {():1,(i,):-1,(i,i):1}
        out=multiply(out,factor)
    return out

def generators(n):
    # Return (name, changed index, image word) with indices 0-based.
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
            if i!=j:
                yield ('K',i,j),i,(j+1,i+1,-j-1)
        for j,k in combinations([j for j in range(n) if j!=i],2):
            yield ('C',i,j,k),i,(i+1,j+1,k+1,-j-1,-k-1)

def check(n):
    basis={(i,j,k) for i in range(n) for j,k in combinations(range(n),2)}
    images=[]
    for name,i,word in generators(n):
        d=expansion(word+(-i-1,));d[()]-=1
        d={u:x for u,x in d.items() if x}
        assert d and all(len(u)==2 for u in d)
        nonzero=[]
        for j,k in combinations(range(n),2):
            a=d.get((j,k),0)
            assert d.get((k,j),0)==-a
            if a: nonzero.append(((i,j,k),a))
        assert len(nonzero)==1
        key,sgn=nonzero[0]
        assert sgn in (-1,1)
        assert d=={(key[1],key[2]):sgn,(key[2],key[1]):-sgn}
        images.append(key)
    assert len(images)==len(set(images))==len(basis)==n*n*(n-1)//2
    assert set(images)==basis
    return len(images)

if __name__=='__main__':
    for n in range(2,13):
        print(n,check(n),'signed-permutation columns checked')
```

```
# Two integral degree-two relations

Write a_i;jk=e_i^* tensor [X_j,X_k] for the signed degree-one Magnus basis.

## Same target

Fix i and put K=<x_j:j!=i>. Define T_u(x_i)=x_i u, fixing K, and P_v(x_i)=x_i, P_v(y)=v y v^{-1} for y in K. Both T:K->Aut(F_n) and P:K->IA_n are homomorphisms. Moreover P_v T_u P_v^{-1}=T_{vuv^{-1}}, so [P_v,T_u]=T_[v,u]=[T_v,T_u]. If u,v in gamma_2(K), then T_u,T_v are in IA and P_v in gamma_2(IA). Therefore [T_v,T_u] lies in gamma_3(IA). This proves every same-target wedge a_i;jk wedge a_i;qr is zero in gr_2(IA), with no restrictions on overlap among j,k,q,r beyond avoidance of i. The exact automorphism identity was checked for all basic commutator tails through n=6 in same_target_relation.py; its displayed algebraic proof is valid for all n.

## Dummy contraction index

Take five distinct i,p,k,q,r. The wedge R=a_i;pk wedge a_q;qr is induced by two exactly commuting Magnus automorphisms. Apply an elementary Nielsen automorphism whose homology action is e_p->e_p+e_q. Its action on the two factors is a_i;pk->a_i;pk+a_i;qk and a_q;qr->a_q;qr-a_p;qr. Subtract R and the also-exact commuting wedge a_i;qk wedge a_p;qr. Since every GL_n(Z) conjugate of a true gr_2 relation is again a true relation, the expansion gives

a_i;pk wedge a_p;qr = a_i;qk wedge a_q;qr in gr_2(IA).

Thus a nonzero derivation bracket column with union support five (exactly one contraction) may be replaced integrally by one supported on four labels. This does NOT by itself prove the whole all-rank kernel is generated in support four: relations between support-four columns can involve more labels.
```

```
# Integral degree-two Johnson injectivity in every rank

## Inputs

Use the proved degree-one identification IA_n^ab=V_n=Hom(H,L_2), with signed basis a_i;jk=e_i^* tensor [X_j,X_k], j!=k. For i in {j,k} its lift is a signed basis conjugation, otherwise a commutator transvection. Let q:Lambda^2 V_n -> gr_2(IA_n) be the surjective bracket map and B:Lambda^2 V_n -> Hom(H,L_3) the derivation bracket, so B=J_2 q.

The exact primitive-lattice certificates degree2_certificate.py prove J_2 injective for n=3,4; rank2 follows from rank2_proof.md. Consequently any z supported on at most four coordinate labels with Bz=0 has qz=0 in every rank: embed the corresponding smaller free-group automorphism group by fixing the extra generators. No injectivity of this group inclusion on lower-central quotients is required.

## Reduction of individual columns

The degree-one basis is multihomogeneous with weight e_j+e_k-e_i; hence Lambda^2 V is a direct sum of integral weight groups and B preserves weight. This is an algebraic multigrading; no assumption that weight-space decomposition follows from the integral diagonal torus is made.

Consider a wedge of two basis derivations. With different targets and no target occurring in the other's output pair, their Magnus lifts commute exactly. With the same target, if both are commutator transvections, their wedge is a true degree-two relation in all ranks by the partial-inner identity proved in agent4_degree2_relations.md. Every remaining column has at least one cross-contraction and its union support has at most five labels. A column supported on exactly five labels has two distinct-target commutator transvections and exactly one contraction; all five labels are distinct and it has form a_i;pk wedge a_p;qr, up to swapping factors/signs. The integral dummy-exchange relation from agent4_degree2_relations.md replaces it by a_i;qk wedge a_q;qr, supported on four labels. Thus every original column is, modulo true relations in ker q, zero or a column supported on at most four labels.

We now canonicalize each resulting column using only relations supported on at most four labels. It suffices to do this weight by weight. The possible nonzero weights in Hom(H,L_3) are (I) exactly one coefficient -1, all others nonnegative; (II) 2e_a; (III) e_a+e_b, a!=b. For any other weight B is zero and every reduced column is a local relation, hence q-zero.

## I: One negative coefficient

Write the weight as -e_i plus three positive letters with multiplicity, none equal to i. The output space is the corresponding multihomogeneous component of the free Lie ring on these positive letters, at target i. If the three letters are distinct a,b,c, an integral basis is [[a,b],c], [[a,c],b]. Both are images, up to sign, of local columns a_i;a c wedge a_a;a b and a_i;a b wedge a_a;a c. If the letters are a,a,b, the output is rank one, generated by [a,[a,b]], which is the image up to sign of a_i;a b wedge a_b;a b. Three identical letters give zero. Choose these columns as canonical lifts of a free basis.

For any reduced column, express its image in this integral basis. Subtract the corresponding canonical lifts. The difference is supported on at most four labels: the actual weight support has size four in the distinct-letter case and size three in the repeated-letter case, while the reduced column has at most four labels and contains the actual weight support. Its image is zero, so local injectivity proves the required equality in gr_2. The canonical images are independent. Therefore J_2 is injective on this weight.

## II: Weight 2e_a

The codomain weight has basis T_i=e_i^*[[X_i,X_a],X_a], i!=a. Every K--K column of this weight consists of two conjugations by X_a and commutes exactly. Every remaining nonzero column has form E_ip=a_i;p a wedge a_p;i a, with i,p distinct from a; its image is -T_i+T_p. Fix any anchor j!=a and take E_ji as canonical star columns, i!=a,j. Their images T_i-T_j are independent. The difference E_ip-(E_jp-E_ji) has zero image and is supported on {a,i,p,j}, at most four labels; terms with repeated indices are interpreted as zero and skew signs. Therefore local injectivity expresses every column in these independent star columns. J_2 is injective on this weight.

## III: Weight e_a+e_b, a!=b

For s outside {a,b}, put U_s=e_s^*[[X_s,X_a],X_b] and V_s=e_s^*[[X_s,X_b],X_a]. Put Z_a=e_a^*[X_a,[X_a,X_b]] and Z_b=e_b^*[[X_a,X_b],X_b]. These form an integral codomain basis. Use the actual columns

W_s=a_s;s a wedge a_s;s b,
L_s=a_s;a b wedge a_a;a s,
G=a_a;a b wedge a_b;a b.

Their images are respectively U_s-V_s, U_s+Z_a, and -Z_a+Z_b. These images are independent and form the full integral kernel of the functional sending every U_s,V_s to +1 and Z_a,Z_b to -1. This follows directly by solving coefficients at U_s,V_s, then at Z_a,Z_b; equivalently the corresponding minors have gcd one.

This functional is the symmetric trace on this weight. Define trace on an associative expansion of D(X_t) by contracting its first letter with t and symmetrizing the remaining two letters. It gives

Tr(e_t^*[[u,v],w])=delta_tu vw-delta_tv uw,
Tr(e_t^*[u,[v,w]])=-delta_tv uw+delta_tw uv.

Substitution into the derivation-bracket formula shows Tr([D,E])=0 for any degree-one derivations D,E; cancellation is termwise. The script agent4_trace_certificate.py verifies this exactly for every pair of degree-one basis derivations through n=8 (41,250 pairs), as well as all W_s,L_s,G signs. The displayed formulas give the general proof independently of these finite tests.

For a reduced column supported on T, with |T|<=4 and {a,b} subset T, its trace-zero image therefore has a unique integral expression in G and those W_s,L_s for s in T minus {a,b}. Subtract that expression from the column. The result has zero image and support within T, so local rank<=4 injectivity makes it a true relation. Thus all columns are expressed in the globally independent G,W_s,L_s. J_2 is injective on this weight.

## Conclusion

Every integral weight has zero Johnson kernel, hence J_2:gr_2(IA_n)->Hom(H,L_3) is injective for every n>=2. Equivalently gamma_2(IA_n) intersect A_3(n)=gamma_3(IA_n). Combined with the degree-one theorem A_2=gamma_2, this proves A_3(n)=gamma_3(IA_n) for all n.

This establishes only c=2 of the requested all-rank low-degree range. It does not establish c=3,4,5 or any high-degree noninjectivity.
```

```
from sage.all import *
from sage.libs.gap.libgap import libgap
import sys,json
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent3_magnus import compose,identity
D=json.loads(Path('workspace/agent3_AutF3_presentation.json').read_text())
F=libgap.FreeGroup(3); A=libgap.AutomorphismGroup(F)
gs=libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(A)
invimgs=[[list(map(int,libgap.LetterRepAssocWord(libgap.Image(g**-1,x)))) for x in libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(F)] for g in gs]
D['inverse_images']=invimgs
G={i+1:tuple(map(tuple,img)) for i,img in enumerate(D['aut_images'])}
G.update({-i-1:tuple(map(tuple,img)) for i,img in enumerate(invimgs)})
for i,r in enumerate(D['relators']):
 out=identity(3)
 for a in r:out=compose(G[a],out)
 assert out==identity(3),(i,out)
 print('exact AutF3 relator verified',i+1,'length',len(r))
Path('workspace/agent3_AutF3_presentation.json').write_text(json.dumps(D,indent=2))
```

```
#103 agent_4:
Structural propagation investigation: no general implication 'first torsion at degree6 => all later degrees'. Countermechanism: torsionfree nilpotent groups can have p-torsion in one graded LCS degree and none later (e.g. rational class3 Lie algebra with central t=[a,b], u=[c,d], [u,e]=p t; suitable integral lattice gives p[t]=0 in gr2 but gr3 torsionfree). from pathlib import Path
```
```
Path('workspace/agent3_finite_magnus.py').write_text('''import numpy as np
from itertools import product
class MagnusRing:
 def __init__(self,d=7,p=3):
  self.d=d;self.p=p
  self.mons=[a for k in range(d+1) for a in product(range(k+1),repeat=3) if sum(a)==k]
  self.idx={a:i for i,a in enumerate(self.mons)};self.deg=np.array(list(map(sum,self.mons)))
  self.N=len(self.mons);self.z=np.zeros((self.N,3,3),dtype=np.int64)
  self.mulidx=np.full((self.N,self.N),-1,dtype=int)
  for i,a in enumerate(self.mons):
   for j,b in enumerate(self.mons):
    c=tuple(x+y for x,y in zip(a,b))
    if sum(c)<=d:self.mulidx[i,j]=self.idx[c]
 def mul(self,A,B):
  ia=np.flatnonzero(np.any(A,axis=(1,2)));ib=np.flatnonzero(np.any(B,axis=(1,2)))
  C=self.z.copy()
  if not len(ia) or not len(ib):return C
  ids=self.mulidx[np.ix_(ia,ib)];r,s=np.where(ids>=0)
  if len(r):np.add.at(C,ids[r,s],np.matmul(A[ia[r]],B[ib[s]]))
  return C%self.p
 def inv(self,A):
  C=self.z.copy();power=A.copy();sgn=-1
  for k in range(1,self.d+1):
   C=(C+sgn*power)%self.p
   power=self.mul(power,A)
   if not power.any():break
   sgn=-sgn
  return C
 def comm(self,A,B,Bi=None):
  C=(self.mul(A,B)-self.mul(B,A))%self.p
  if not C.any():return C
  C=(C+self.mul(C,self.inv(A)))%self.p
  return (C+self.mul(C,self.inv(B) if Bi is None else Bi))%self.p
 def scalar(self,exps):
  C=self.z.copy()
  for a,v in exps.items():C[self.idx[a]]=np.eye(3,dtype=int)*v
  return C%self.p
 def generators(self):
  # Abelianized Fox Jacobian, columns = changed images; IA composition
  # induces matrix multiplication up to opposite convention, irrelevant here.
  gs=[]
  for i in range(3):
   for j in range(3):
    if i==j:continue
    A=self.z.copy();ej=tuple(int(k==j) for k in range(3));ei=tuple(int(k==i) for k in range(3))
    A[self.idx[ej],i,i]+=1;A[self.idx[ei],j,i]-=1;gs.append(A%self.p)
   js=[j for j in range(3) if j!=i];j,k=js
   A=self.z.copy()
   ej=tuple(int(t==j) for t in range(3));ek=tuple(int(t==k) for t in range(3));ei=tuple(int(t==i) for t in range(3))
   eik=tuple(x+y for x,y in zip(ei,ek));eij=tuple(x+y for x,y in zip(ei,ej))
   A[self.idx[ek],j,i]-=1;A[self.idx[eik],j,i]-=1
   A[self.idx[ej],k,i]+=1;A[self.idx[eij],k,i]+=1;gs.append(A%self.p)
  return gs
class Span:
 def __init__(self,R):self.R=R;self.basis={}
 def add(self,A):
  v=A.reshape(-1).copy()%self.R.p
  for pivot,b in sorted(self.basis.items()):
   if v[pivot]:v=(v-v[pivot]*b)%self.R.p
  nz=np.flatnonzero(v)
  if not len(nz):return None
  pivot=int(nz[0]);v=v*pow(int(v[pivot]),-1,self.R.p)%self.R.p
  self.basis[pivot]=v
  return v.reshape(A.shape)
 def arrays(self):return [b.reshape(self.R.z.shape) for b in self.basis.values()]
''')
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent3_tensor_seeds.py').write_text('''import sys,time
import numpy as np
from itertools import product
from agent3_magnus import K,C,auto_commutator

def expand(w,d=8,p=3):
 starts=[(3**k-1)//2 for k in range(d+2)]
 A=np.zeros(starts[d+1],dtype=np.int8);A[0]=1
 for a in w:
  i=abs(a)-1
  for k in (range(d,0,-1) if a>0 else range(1,d+1)):
   # All words of length k with final letter i, versus their prefixes.
   dest=slice(starts[k]+i,starts[k+1],3);src=slice(starts[k-1],starts[k])
   A[dest]=(A[dest]+(1 if a>0 else -1)*A[src])%p
 return A

def seeds():
 G=[]
 for i in range(1,4):
  for j in range(1,4):
   if i!=j:G.append(K(3,i,j))
  js=[j for j in range(1,4) if j!=i];G.append(C(3,i,*js))
 G2=[]
 for (a,ai),(b,bi) in product(G,repeat=2):
  h=auto_commutator(a,ai,b,bi);hi=auto_commutator(b,bi,a,ai);G2.append((h,hi))
 G3=[]
 for (a,ai),(b,bi) in product(G2,G):G3.append(auto_commutator(a,ai,b,bi))
 N=(3**9-1)//2
 out=np.zeros((len(G3),3,N),dtype=np.int8)
 cache={};t=time.time()
 for k,g in enumerate(G3):
  for i,w in enumerate(g):
   if w not in cache:cache[w]=expand(w)
   out[k,i]=cache[w]
   out[k,i,0]-=1;out[k,i,1+i]-=1;out[k,i]%=3
  assert not out[k,:,:40].any() # no displacement degree<4
  if k%100==0:print(k,'elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 np.savez_compressed('workspace/agent3_gamma3_tensor_seeds.npz',seeds=out[:,:,(3**4-1)//2:])
 print('saved',out.shape,'elapsed',time.time()-t)
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':seeds()
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
import agent3_tensor_seeds
agent3_tensor_seeds.seeds()
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent3_tensor_commutators.py').write_text('''"""Exact F3 commutator corrections for F in A3, truncation wordlength8.
Input f displacement output-major degrees4..8; Lg is action g^-1 F g linear part.
Returns [F,g^-1] displacement in degrees5..8 (a true gamma4 element).
"""
import numpy as np

def quad_g(f4,index):
 # generator ordering K(i,j) jascending followed C(i;j,k), i=0,1,2.
 i=index//3;t=index%3;others=[j for j in range(3) if j!=i]
 if t<2:
  j=others[t];a,b=f4[j],f4[i]
 else:
  j,k=others;a,b=f4[j],f4[k]
 Q=np.zeros((3,6561),dtype=np.int16)
 Q[i]=(np.outer(a,b)-np.outer(b,a)).reshape(-1)%3
 return Q

def directional_5_on_4(direction5,f4):
 # derivative direction applied to f4, homogeneous wordlength8
 out=np.zeros((3,6561),dtype=np.int16)
 for i in range(3):
  H=f4[i].reshape((3,)*4)
  for pos in range(4):
   for j in range(3):
    h=np.take(H,j,axis=pos)
    v=direction5[j].reshape((3,)*5)
    C=np.tensordot(h,v,axes=0)
    perm=list(range(pos))+list(range(3,8))+list(range(pos,3))
    out[i]+=C.transpose(perm).reshape(-1)
 return out%3

def commutator_seed(f,linear_h,index):
 f=f.reshape(3,9801).astype(np.int16)
 h=linear_h.reshape(3,9801).astype(np.int16).copy()%3
 f4=f[:,:81]
 h[:,-6561:]=(h[:,-6561:]+quad_g(f4,index))%3
 direction5=(h[:,81:324]-f[:,81:324])%3
 corr=directional_5_on_4(direction5,f4)
 out=(f-h)%3
 out[:,-6561:]=(out[:,-6561:]+corr)%3
 assert not out[:,:81].any()
 return out[:,81:].astype(np.int8)
''')
print('saved commutator helper')
```

```
import sys,json,time
from pathlib import Path
from itertools import product
from collections import defaultdict
import numpy as np
from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix,eye,kron,save_npz
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent3_magnus import K,C,magnus
D=8; LO=4
WORDS=[w for l in range(LO,D+1) for w in product(range(3),repeat=l)]
IX={w:i for i,w in enumerate(WORDS)}; M=len(WORDS); N=3*M
LENS=np.array([len(w) for w in WORDS])
def clean(P):return {w:int(c)%3 for w,c in P.items() if c%3}
def mul(P,Q):
 out=defaultdict(int)
 for u,c in P.items():
  for v,e in Q.items():
   if len(u)+len(v)<=D:out[u+v]+=c*e
 return clean(out)
def poly(w):
 P=magnus(w,D);P.pop((),None)
 return {tuple(i-1 for i in u):c%3 for u,c in P.items() if c%3}
def spclean(A):
 A=A.tocsr();A.sum_duplicates();A.data%=3;A.eliminate_zeros();return A

def operator(G,H):
 # P is scalar polynomial substitution by H; build cache for every prefix.
 cache={():{():1}}
 rows=[];cols=[];vals=[]
 for l in range(1,D+1):
  for w in product(range(3),repeat=l):
   Q=mul(cache[w[:-1]],H[w[-1]])
   cache[w]=Q
   if l>=LO:
    col=IX[w]
    for u,c in Q.items():rows.append(IX[u]);cols.append(col);vals.append(c)
 P=coo_matrix((np.array(vals,dtype=np.int32),(rows,cols)),shape=(M,M)).tocsr()
 rows=[];cols=[];vals=[]
 for i,Gi in enumerate(G):
  for v,c in Gi.items():
   maxlen=D-len(v)+1
   for pos,j in enumerate(v):
    for l in range(LO,maxlen+1):
     for w in product(range(3),repeat=l):
      u=v[:pos]+w+v[pos+1:]
      rows.append(i*M+IX[u]);cols.append(j*M+IX[w]);vals.append(c)
 J=coo_matrix((np.array(vals,dtype=np.int32),(rows,cols)),shape=(N,N)).tocsr()
 out=spclean(kron(eye(3,dtype=np.int32),P,format='csr')@J-eye(N,dtype=np.int32))
 rr,cc=out.nonzero();assert np.all(LENS[rr%M]>LENS[cc%M])
 return out

def substitute_poly(P,H):
 cache={():{():1}}
 def ev(w):
  if w not in cache:cache[w]=mul(ev(w[:-1]),H[w[-1]])
  return cache[w]
 out=defaultdict(int)
 for w,c in P.items():
  for u,e in ev(w).items():out[u]+=c*e
 return clean(out)
def compose_poly(F,G):return tuple(substitute_poly(P,F) for P in G)
gens=[]; labels=[]
for i in range(1,4):
 for j in range(1,4):
  if i!=j:gens.append(K(3,i,j));labels.append(['K',i,j])
 other=[j for j in range(1,4) if j!=i]
 gens.append(C(3,i,*other));labels.append(['C',i,*other])
meta={'degree':D,'min_degree':LO,'word_order':'length then lex, letters 0,1,2','vector_order':'output-major, output*M+word_index','M':M,'labels':labels,'orientation':'N_g f = displacement(g^-1 F g)-f to linear order in f','polynomials':[]}
for k,(g,gi) in enumerate(gens):
 for invflag in (False,True):
  gg,hh=(gi,g) if invflag else (g,gi)
  G=tuple(poly(w) for w in gg);H=tuple(poly(w) for w in hh)
  start=time.time();A=operator(G,H)
  filename=f'workspace/agent4_tensor_N_{k}'+('_inv' if invflag else '')+'.npz'
  save_npz(filename,A)
  # Direct exact substitution check on arbitrary order-4 displacement (wordlength 5).
  for i in range(3):
   w=tuple((i+j)%3 for j in range(5))
   F=[{(j,):1} for j in range(3)];F[i][w]=1
   actual=compose_poly(H,compose_poly(tuple(F),G))
   vec=np.zeros(N,dtype=np.int32);vec[i*M+IX[w]]=1
   predicted=(A@vec+vec)%3
   out=np.zeros(N,dtype=np.int32)
   for j,P0 in enumerate(actual):
    P0=dict(P0);P0[(j,)]=(P0.get((j,),0)-1)%3
    for u,c in P0.items():
     if c:assert len(u)>=LO;out[j*M+IX[u]]=c
   assert np.array_equal(out,predicted)
  print(k,invflag,'nnz',A.nnz,'seconds',round(time.time()-start,2),flush=True)
  meta['polynomials'].append({'generator':k,'inverse':invflag,'G':[[[list(w),c] for w,c in P.items()] for P in G],'H':[[[list(w),c] for w,c in P.items()] for P in H],'file':filename})
Path('workspace/agent4_tensor_operators.json').write_text(json.dumps(meta))
```

```
Path('workspace/agent3_howell9.py').write_text('''"""Exact module generators and cardinality over Z/9, dense Sage BLAS.
No saturation or division by nonunits is assumed.
"""
from sage.all import *
import numpy as np,gc,time
_fun,_args=matrix(GF(3),[[1]]).__reduce__()
def fastmat(A,p=3):
 a=list(_args);a[1]=MatrixSpace(Integers(p),*A.shape);a[4]=(1,True,A.astype(np.uint8,copy=False).tobytes());return _fun(*a)
def tonumpy(M):return np.frombuffer(M.__reduce__()[1][4][2],dtype=np.uint8).reshape(M.nrows(),M.ncols())
def module9(A,verbose=True):
 t=time.time();A=np.asarray(A,dtype=np.uint8)%9
 P=fastmat(A%3);PT=P.transpose();rows=list(PT.pivots());del PT
 cols=list(P.pivots());r=len(cols);del P;gc.collect()
 if verbose:print('module9 mod3rank',r,'elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 if not r:
  assert not (A%3).any();E=fastmat(A//3);E.echelonize();s=E.rank();B=3*tonumpy(E[:s,:]);return B,(0,s)
 C=A[rows,:];U=C[:,cols]
 V=fastmat(U%3).inverse();V9=fastmat(tonumpy(V),9);del V
 U9=fastmat(U,9)
 V9=V9*(2*identity_matrix(Integers(9),r)-U9*V9);del U9
 C9=V9*fastmat(C,9);del V9,C,U;gc.collect()
 assert C9.matrix_from_columns(cols)==identity_matrix(Integers(9),r)
 if verbose:print('module9 normalized','elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 R9=fastmat(A,9)-fastmat(A[:,cols],9)*C9;del A;gc.collect()
 RR=tonumpy(R9);assert not (RR%3).any()
 E=fastmat(RR//3);del R9,RR;gc.collect()
 E.echelonize();s=E.rank();Earr=tonumpy(E[:s,:]);del E
 B=np.vstack((tonumpy(C9),3*Earr)).astype(np.uint8);del C9;gc.collect()
 if verbose:print('module9 ranks',r,s,'log3size',2*r+s,'elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 return B,(r,s)
''')
```

```
        SetSize( G, infinity );
    elif n = 10 then
        Info( InfoLPRES, 1, "Baumslag's group" );
        F := FreeGroup( "a", "b", "t", "u" );
        a := F.1;
        b := F.2;
        t := F.3;
        u := F.4;
        rels := [ u / b ];
        endos := [ GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b, t, u ], [ a, b, t, u ^ t ] ), GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b, t, u ], [ a, b, t, u ^ (t ^ -1) ] ) ];
        itrels := [ a ^ t / a ^ 4, (b ^ 2) ^ t / b, Comm( a, u ) ];
        G := LPresentedGroup( F, rels, endos, itrels );
        SetIsInvariantLPresentation( G, false );
        SetSize( G, infinity );
    elif n = 11 then
        Info( InfoLPRES, 1, "The modified L-presentation of the Basilica Group" );
        F := FreeGroup( "a", "b" );
        a := F.1;
        b := F.2;
        rels := [  ];
        endos := [ GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b ], [ b ^ 2, a ] ), GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b ], [ a * b, a ^ 2 ] ) ];
        itrels := [ Comm( a, a ^ b ) ];
        G := LPresentedGroup( F, rels, endos, itrels );
        SetSize( G, infinity );
    elif n = 12 then
        Info( InfoLPRES, 1, "The Hanoi-Tower group from [BSZ09]" );
        F := FreeGroup( "a", "b", "c" );
        a := F.1;
        b := F.2;
        c := F.3;
        d := Comm( a, b );
        e := Comm( b, c );
        f := Comm( c, a );
        g := d ^ c;
        h := e ^ a;
        i := f ^ b;
        rels := [ a ^ 2, b ^ 2, c ^ 2 ];
        endos := [ GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b, c ], [ a, b ^ c, c ^ b ] ) ];
        itrels := [ d ^ -1 * e * f * i ^ -1 * g * e, h * e ^ -1 * d ^ -1 * f * d * i ^ -1, e ^ -1 * g ^ -1 * f ^ -1 * e * g * f, e ^ -1 * d * h * e ^ -2 * d ^ -1 * h ^ 2, h * g * d ^ -2 * f ^ -1 * g * f * e ^ -1 ];
        G := LPresentedGroup( F, rels, endos, itrels );
        SetIsInvariantLPresentation( G, true );
        SetSize( G, infinity );
    else
```

```
import numpy as np,gc,time
from agent3_howell9 import fastmat,tonumpy,module9

def normalize27(C,cols=None):
 if cols is None:cols=list(fastmat(C%3).pivots())
 r=len(C);assert len(cols)==r
 U=C[:,cols];V=fastmat(U%3).inverse();V27=fastmat(tonumpy(V),27);del V
 U27=fastmat(U,27);I=identity_matrix(Integers(27),r)
 for _ in range(2):V27=V27*(2*I-U27*V27)
 C27=V27*fastmat(C,27)
 assert C27.matrix_from_columns(cols)==I
 return tonumpy(C27),cols

def module27(A,verbose=True):
 t=time.time();A=np.asarray(A,dtype=np.uint8)%27
 P=fastmat(A%3);PT=P.transpose();rows=list(PT.pivots());del PT
 cols=list(P.pivots());r=len(cols);del P;gc.collect()
 if verbose:print('module27 mod3rank',r,'elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 if not r:
  assert not (A%3).any();E,inv=module9(A//3,False);return 3*E,(0,)+inv
 U,cols=normalize27(A[rows,:],cols);U27=fastmat(U,27)
 if verbose:print('module27 normalized','elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 R27=fastmat(A,27)-fastmat(A[:,cols],27)*U27
 RR=tonumpy(R27);assert not (RR%3).any()
 E,inv=module9(RR//3,False)
 B=np.vstack((U,3*E)).astype(np.uint8)
 if verbose:print('module27 ranks',(r,)+inv,'elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 return B,(r,)+inv
''')
```

```
  nz=np.argwhere(A%3)

  row,col=map(int,nz[0]);u=A[row].copy();u=u*pow(int(u[col]),-1,9)%9
```

```
"""Exact all-rank Magnus-word conjugation formulas, checked as free automorphisms."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from agent2_nielsen_actions import magnus
from degree2_certificate import compose,red

def actions(n):
 assert n>=3
 data3=json.loads(Path('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json').read_text())
 id3,g3,gi3,names3=magnus(3)
 ident,gs,gis,names=magnus(n);index={tuple(v):i+1 for i,v in enumerate(names)}
 def coordinate(typ,i,j,k=None):
  if typ=='K':return index[('K',i,j)]
  if j<k:return index[('C',i,j,k)]
  return -index[('C',i,k,j)]
 def lift(w,labels):
  out=[]
  for x in w:
   nm=names3[abs(x)-1]
   if nm[0]=='K':y=coordinate('K',labels[nm[1]],labels[nm[2]])
   else:y=coordinate('C',labels[nm[1]],labels[nm[2]],labels[nm[3]])
   out.append(y if x>0 else -y)
  return tuple(out)
 def evaluate(w):
  f=ident
  for x in w:f=compose(f,gs[x-1] if x>0 else gis[-x-1])
  return f
 acts={}
 # T sends x0 -> x0*x1.
 t=list(ident);ti=list(ident);t[0]=(1,2);ti[0]=(1,-2);words=[]
 for num,nm in enumerate(names,1):
  support=set(nm[1:]);union=support|{0,1}
  if len(union)<=3:
   extra=next((x for x in union if x not in (0,1)),next(x for x in range(n) if x not in (0,1)))
   labels=[0,1,extra];back={v:i for i,v in enumerate(labels)}
   if nm[0]=='K':idx3=next(i for i,v in enumerate(names3) if v==('K',back[nm[1]],back[nm[2]]));sgn=1
   else:
    a,b=back[nm[2]],back[nm[3]];sgn=1 if a<b else -1
    idx3=next(i for i,v in enumerate(names3) if v==('C',back[nm[1]],min(a,b),max(a,b)))
   w=lift(data3['actions']['T']['words'][idx3],labels)
   if sgn<0:w=tuple(-x for x in reversed(w))
  elif nm[0]=='K' or 0 not in support and 1 not in support:
   w=(num,)
  else:
   _,i,j,k=nm
   if i==0:
    z=coordinate('K',0,1);w=(-z,num,z)
```

```
import numpy as np
from collections import deque
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent3_magnus import compose,identity,inverse,auto_commutator

def abel(f):
 A=np.zeros((3,3),dtype=np.int64)
 for j,w in enumerate(f):
  for a in w:A[abs(a)-1,j]+=1 if a>0 else -1
 return A

def invmat(A):
 a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h,i=map(int,A.reshape(-1))
 C=np.array([[e*i-f*h,c*h-b*i,b*f-c*e],[f*g-d*i,a*i-c*g,c*d-a*f],[d*h-e*g,b*g-a*h,a*e-b*d]],dtype=np.int64)
 det=a*(e*i-f*h)-b*(d*i-f*g)+c*(d*h-e*g)
 assert abs(det)==1
 return C*det

def key(A):return tuple(map(int,A.reshape(-1)))
def build():
 data=json.loads(Path('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json').read_text())
 labels=['T','P','I','C'];aut={};mats={}
 for i,l in enumerate(labels,1):

   aut[s]=tuple(map(tuple,data['actions'][l][typ]));mats[s]=abel(aut[s])
 ident=np.eye(3,dtype=np.int64);words={key(ident):()};queue=deque([(ident,())])
 pool=[1,-1,2,3,4,-4]
 while queue:
  M,w=queue.popleft()
  if len(w)==5:continue
  for a in pool:
   N=M@mats[a];k=key(N)
   if k not in words:words[k]=w+(a,);queue.append((N,w+(a,)))
 print('matrixball5',len(words),flush=True)

 out=[]
 for M in D['generator_matrices']:
  M=np.array(M,dtype=np.int64);best=words.get(key(M))
  if best is None:
   for k,w in words.items():
    N=np.array(k,dtype=np.int64).reshape(3,3);v=words.get(key(M@invmat(N)))
    if v is not None and (best is None or len(v)+len(w)<len(best)):best=v+w
  assert best is not None
  f=identity(3);fi=identity(3)

 result={'action_order':labels,'lifts':out,'relators':D['relators'],'relator_auts':rels}

 return result
```

```
"""Exact free-group checks of Magnus-generator expressions for Aut conjugation."""
import sys,json
from pathlib import Path
from degree2_certificate import compose,inv,red
from degree1_certificate import generators

def magnus(n):
 ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));gs=[];gis=[];names=[]
 for name,i,w in generators(n):
  g=list(ident);g[i]=w;gs.append(tuple(g));names.append(name)
  if name[0]=='K':
   j=name[2];wi=(-j-1,i+1,j+1)
  else:
   j,k=name[2:];wi=(i+1,k+1,j+1,-k-1,-j-1)
  gi=list(ident);gi[i]=wi;gis.append(tuple(gi))
  assert compose(g,gi)==ident and compose(gi,g)==ident
 return ident,gs,gis,names

def expressions(n=3,radius=4):
 ident,gs,gis,names=magnus(n);m=len(gs)
 letters=[(i+1,g,gis[i]) for i,g in enumerate(gs)]+[(-i-1,gis[i],g) for i,g in enumerate(gs)]
 table={ident:((),ident)};frontier=[ident]
 for depth in range(1,radius+1):
  new=[]
  for f in frontier:
   w,fi=table[f]
   for x,g,gi in letters:
    if w and w[-1]==-x:continue
    h=compose(f,g)
    if h in table:continue
    table[h]=(w+(x,),compose(gi,fi));new.append(h)
  frontier=new;print('radius',depth,'size',len(table),flush=True)
 # Standard elementary transvection, swap, inversion, cycle.
 acts=[]
 t=list(ident);ti=list(ident);t[0]=(1,2);ti[0]=(1,-2)
 acts.append(('T',tuple(t),tuple(ti)))
 t=list(ident);t[0],t[1]=t[1],t[0];acts.append(('P',tuple(t),tuple(t)))
 t=list(ident);t[0]=(-1,);acts.append(('I',tuple(t),tuple(t)))
 cyc=tuple(((i+1)%n+1,) for i in range(n));cyci=tuple(((i-1)%n+1,) for i in range(n))
 acts.append(('C',cyc,cyci))
 result={}
 def evaluate(w):
  f=ident
  for x in w:f=compose(f,gs[x-1] if x>0 else gis[-x-1])
  return f
 for name,t,ti in acts:
  assert compose(t,ti)==ident and compose(ti,t)==ident
  words=[]
  for k,g in enumerate(gs):
   target=compose(t,compose(g,ti));answer=None
   if target in table:answer=table[target][0]
   else:
    for b,(bw,bi) in table.items():
     a=compose(target,bi)
     if a in table:
      answer=red(table[a][0]+bw);break
   if answer is None:raise ValueError(('not found',name,k))
   assert evaluate(answer)==target
   words.append(answer)
   print('action',name,'generator',k+1,'word',answer,flush=True)
  result[name]={'auto':t,'inverse':ti,'words':words}
 data={'n':n,'generators':gs,'inverses':gis,'names':names,'actions':result}
 Path('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n%d.json'%n).write_text(json.dumps(data))
 print('All conjugation words checked exactly',flush=True)
 return data
if __name__=='__main__':expressions(int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 3)
```

```
for lam in (1,3,6):
 beam=[(F,(),v)];seen={F};found=None;t=time.time()
 for dep in range(1,35):
  candidates={}
  for f,path,vv in beam:
   for a,g,gi in letters:
    if path and path[-1]==-a:continue
    h=compose(gi,f)

    w=path+(a,);vn=list(vv);vn[abs(a)-1]-=1 if a>0 else -1;vn=tuple(vn)

   if found:break

 if found:
```

```
CVEC_NewCVecClass function ( p, d, len )
    local bestgrease, bitsperel, cl, elsperword, filts, greasetabl, j, l, po, pos, pos2, q, s, scafam, size, tab1, tab2, ty, wordlen, filtscmat;
    if d <= 0 then
        Error( "CVEC_NewCVecClass: Degree must be positive." );
        return fail;
    fi;
    if d >= 1024 then
        Error( "CVEC_NewCVecClass: Degree must be < ", 1024, "." );
        return fail;
    fi;
    if not IsPrime( p ) then
        Error( "CVEC_MakeField: p must be a prime." );
        return fail;
    fi;
    if not IsSmallIntRep( p ) then
        Error( "CVEC_NewCVecClass: p must be a prime that is an immediate int ." );
        return fail;
    fi;
    if not IsSmallIntRep( len ) then
        Error( "CVEC_NewCVecClass: len must be an immediate integer." );
        return fail;
    fi;
    q := p ^ d;
    if q <= 65536 then
        size := 0;
    elif IsSmallIntRep( q ) then
        size := 1;
    else
        size := 2;
    fi;
    pos := Position( CVEC_q, q );
    if pos = fail then
        l := [  ];
        l[1] := p;
        l[2] := d;
        l[3] := q;
        l[13] := size;
        po := - CoefficientsOfLaurentPolynomial( ConwayPolynomial( p, d ) );
        if po[2] <> 0 then
            Error( "Unexpected case #1" );
        fi;
        po := List( po[1], IntFFE );
        s := CVEC_COEFF_LIST_TO_C( po, "" );
        l[4] := s;
        if IsOddInt( p ) then
            bitsperel := 1;
        else
            bitsperel := 0;
        fi;
        j := p - 1;
        while j > 0 do
            bitsperel := bitsperel + 1;
            j := QuoInt( j, 2 );
        od;
        l[5] := bitsperel;
        elsperword := QuoInt( 32, bitsperel );
        if 8 = 8 then
            elsperword := elsperword * 2;
        fi;
        l[6] := elsperword;
        if q <= 16 and IsBound( CVEC_BestGreaseTab[q] ) then
            bestgrease := CVEC_BestGreaseTab[q];
            greasetabl := q ^ bestgrease;
        elif q <= 256 then
            bestgrease := 1;
            greasetabl := q;
        else
            bestgrease := 0;
            greasetabl := 0;
        fi;
        l[8] := bestgrease;
        l[9] := greasetabl;
        filts := IsCVecRep;
        filtscmat := IsCMatRep;
        if size = 0 then
            filts := filts and IsCVecRepOverSmallField;
            filtscmat := filtscmat and IsCVecRepOverSmallField;
        fi;
        if d = 1 then
            filts := filts and IsCVecRepOverPrimeField;
            filtscmat := filtscmat and IsCVecRepOverPrimeField;
        fi;
        l[10] := filts;
        l[15] := filtscmat;
        scafam := FFEFamily( p );
        l[14] := scafam;
        if q <= 65536 then
            tab1 := 0 * [ 1 .. q ];
            tab2 := 0 * [ 1 .. q ];
            CVEC_INIT_SMALL_GFQ_TABS( p, ConwayPol( p, d ), tab1, tab2 );
        else
            if p < 65536 then
                tab1 := 0 * [ 1 .. p ];
                tab2 := 0 * [ 1 .. p ];
                CVEC_INIT_SMALL_GFQ_TABS( p, ConwayPol( p, 1 ), tab1, tab2 );
            else
                tab1 := [  ];
                tab2 := [  ];
            fi;
        fi;
        l[11] := tab1;
        l[12] := tab2;
        Objectify( NewType( CVecFieldInfoFamily, IsCVecFieldInfo ), l );
        CVEC_FINALIZE_FIELDINFO( l );
        pos := PositionSorted( CVEC_q, q );
        Add( CVEC_q, q, pos );
        Add( CVEC_F, l, pos );
        Add( CVEC_lens, [  ], pos );
        Add( CVEC_classes, [  ], pos );
    else
        elsperword := CVEC_F[pos]![6];
        filts := CVEC_F[pos]![10];
        filtscmat := CVEC_F[pos]![15];
        scafam := CVEC_F[pos]![14];
    fi;
    pos2 := Position( CVEC_lens[pos], len );
    if pos2 <> fail then
        return CVEC_classes[pos][pos2];
    fi;
    cl := [  ];
    cl[1] := CVEC_F[pos];
    cl[2] := len;
    wordlen := d * QuoInt( len + elsperword - 1, elsperword );
    cl[3] := wordlen;
    ty := NewType( CollectionsFamily( scafam ), filts and IsMutable, cl );
    cl[4] := ty;
    cl[5] := GF( p, d );
    cl[6] := CVEC_lens[pos];

    Objectify( NewType( CVecClassFamily, IsCVecClass ), cl );
    pos2 := PositionSorted( CVEC_lens[pos], len );
```

```
function ( basis, vec, extend, dec )
    local c, firstnz, j, lc, len, lev, mo, newpiv, pivs;
    if dec <> fail then
        MultVector( dec, Zero( dec[1] ) );
    fi;
    firstnz := PositionNonZero( vec );
    if firstnz > Length( vec ) then
        return true;
    fi;
    len := Length( basis!.vectors );
    if len = 1 and IsPlistRep( basis!.vectors ) then
        ConvertToMatrixRep( basis!.vectors );
    fi;
    for j in [ 1 .. len ] do
        if basis!.pivots[j] >= firstnz then
            c := vec[basis!.pivots[j]];
            if not IsZero( c ) then
                if dec <> fail then
                    dec[j] := c;
                fi;
                AddRowVector( vec, basis!.vectors[j], - c );
            fi;
        fi;
    od;
    newpiv := PositionNonZero( vec );
    if newpiv = Length( vec ) + 1 then
        return true;
    else
        if extend then
            c := vec[newpiv];
            if dec <> fail then
                dec[len + 1] := c;
            fi;
            MultVector( vec, c ^ -1 );
            Add( basis!.vectors, vec );
            Add( basis!.pivots, newpiv );
        fi;
        return false;
    fi;
    return;
end
```

## response 216

content:
```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent3_cvec_basis.py').write_text('''"""Memory-efficient exact F3 rowspace via GAP CVEC (3 bits/coefficient).
NumPy exchange uses portable CVEC external packed32-bit words, 10coeffs/word.
Binary IO uses GAP IO_Write, not PrintTo (which strips NULs).
"""
from sage.all import *
from sage.libs.gap.libgap import libgap
import numpy as np
libgap.eval('LoadPackage("cvec",false:OnlyNeeded:=true)')
libgap.eval('LoadPackage("io",false:OnlyNeeded:=true)')
EXPORT=libgap.eval('''+"'''"+'''function(B,filename) local f,v,s; f:=IO_File(filename,"w"); for v in B!.vectors do s:=""; CVEC_CVEC_TO_EXTREP(v,s); IO_Write(f,s); od; IO_Close(f); return B!.pivots;end'''+"'''"+''')
INFO=libgap.eval('function(B)return [Length(B!.vectors),B!.pivots];end')
ESCAPES=['\\\\%03o'%b for b in range(256)]
class CVecBasis:
 def __init__(self,n):
  self.n=n;self.cl=libgap.CVecClass(3,1,n)
  self.basis=libgap.EmptySemiEchelonBasis(libgap.CMat([],self.cl))
 def insert(self,row):
  a=np.asarray(row,dtype=np.uint32)%3
  if len(a)!=self.n:raise ValueError('wrong length')
  a=np.pad(a,(0,(-len(a))%10))
  packed=(a.reshape(-1,10)<<np.arange(0,30,3,dtype=np.uint32)).sum(axis=1,dtype=np.uint32).astype('<u4').tobytes()
  s=libgap.eval('"'+''.join(ESCAPES[b] for b in packed)+'"')
  v=libgap.CVec(self.cl);libgap.CVEC_EXTREP_TO_CVEC(s,v)
  return not bool(libgap.CleanRow(self.basis,v,True,libgap.fail))
 def info(self):return INFO(self.basis).sage()
 def export(self,filename):
  piv=EXPORT(self.basis,filename).sage();return piv
 def numpy(self,filename):
  piv=self.export(filename);raw=np.fromfile(filename,dtype='<u4').reshape(len(piv),-1)
  A=((raw[:,:,None]>>np.arange(0,30,3,dtype=np.uint32))&7).astype(np.uint8).reshape(len(piv),-1)[:,:self.n]
  assert np.all(A<3)
  return A,piv
''')
import sys,numpy as np,time
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent3_cvec_basis import CVecBasis
from sage.all import *
A=np.random.default_rng(42).integers(0,3,(100,1000),dtype=np.uint8)
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent3_d9_detector.py').write_text('''"""Exact mod3 tensor wordlength9 detector using GAP CVEC rowspaces.
Uses actual gamma4 logarithm seeds and proven class2 logarithm arithmetic.
Queue rows are original independent generators, not reduced basis vectors.
"""
import sys,os,json,time,gc
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from scipy.sparse import load_npz
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent3_cvec_basis import CVecBasis,libgap
NCO=88209;CAP=16000;BATCH=128
ix=np.array([i*29484+j for i in range(3) for j in range(81,29484)])
Ns=[load_npz(f'workspace/agent4_tensor_d9_N_{j}.npz')[ix,:][:,ix] for j in range(9)]

def insert(B,Q,n,row):
 if not np.any(row):return n
 if B.insert(row):
  if n>=CAP:raise RuntimeError('queue capacity exceeded')
  Q[n]=row;n+=1
 return n

def finish(Q,n,c):
 Q.flush();del Q
 filename=f'workspace/agent3_d9_gamma{c}_queue.raw'
 with open(filename,'r+b') as f:f.truncate(n*NCO)
 json.dump({'rows':n,'cols':NCO},open(filename+'.json','w'))
 return np.memmap(filename,dtype=np.uint8,mode='r',shape=(n,NCO))

def initqueue(c):
 return np.memmap(f'workspace/agent3_d9_gamma{c}_queue.raw',dtype=np.uint8,mode='w+',shape=(CAP,NCO))

def gamma4():
 t=time.time();B=CVecBasis(NCO);Q=initqueue(4);n=0
 for j in range(9):
  A=np.load(f'workspace/agent4_gamma4_d9_log_seeds_{j}.npz')['seeds'].reshape(729,NCO)
  for row in A:n=insert(B,Q,n,row)
  print('D9 gamma4 seedblock',j,'rank',n,'elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 del A
 cur=0
 while cur<n:
  end=min(cur+BATCH,n);A=np.array(Q[cur:end])
  for N in Ns:
   C=((N@A.T).T%3).astype(np.uint8)
   for row in C:n=insert(B,Q,n,row)
  cur=end
  if cur%1024==0 or cur==n:print('D9 gamma4 closurequeue',cur,'/',n,'elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 print('D9 GAMMA4 FINAL',n,'elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 out=finish(Q,n,4);del B;gc.collect();libgap.GASMAN('collect')
 return out

def nextgamma(A,c):
 t=time.time();B=CVecBasis(NCO);Q=initqueue(c);n=0
 for start in range(0,len(A),BATCH):
  V=np.array(A[start:start+BATCH])
  for N in Ns:
   C=((N@V.T).T%3).astype(np.uint8)
   for row in C:n=insert(B,Q,n,row)
  if start%1024==0:print('D9 gamma',c,'input',start,'rank',n,'elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 print('D9 GAMMA',c,'FINAL',n,'elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 out=finish(Q,n,c);del B;gc.collect();libgap.GASMAN('collect')
 return out

def leadingrank(A,c):
 starts=[(3**k-1)//2 for k in range(11)]
 cols=[i*29403+j for i in range(3) for j in range(starts[c+1]-starts[5],starts[c+2]-starts[5])]
 B=CVecBasis(len(cols))
 for start in range(0,len(A),BATCH):
  V=A[start:start+BATCH,cols]
  for row in V:
   if np.any(row):B.insert(row)
 r=B.info()[0];del B;gc.collect();libgap.GASMAN('collect');return r

if __name__=='__main__':
 A=gamma4()
 for c in (5,6,7):
  A=nextgamma(A,c);print('D9 leading rank',c,leadingrank(A,c),flush=True)
''')

 A=D.nextgamma(A,c);print('D9 leading rank',c,D.leadingrank(A,c),flush=True)
```

```
"""Compact exact invariant-L-presentation seeds for central IA3 cover.
Signed-permutation chunks are shortened modulo explicitly imposed B3 relations.
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from degree2_certificate import compose,red,inv

def generate(limit=2000000):
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))
 iw=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_inverse_words_n3.json'))
 wd=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_magnus_aut_words_n3.json'))
 ar=json.load(open('workspace/agent4_AutF3_four_generator_presentation.json'))
 order=['T','P','I','C'];mp={};aa={};ident=((1,),(2,),(3,))
 for i,s in enumerate(order,1):
  mp[i]=[tuple(w) for w in d['actions'][s]['words']];mp[-i]=[tuple(w) for w in iw[s]]
  aa[i]=tuple(map(tuple,d['actions'][s]['auto']));aa[-i]=tuple(map(tuple,d['actions'][s]['inverse']))
 def eva(w):
  f=ident
  for a in w:f=compose(f,aa[a])
  return f
 trans={ident:()};front=[ident]
 while front:
  new=[]
  for f in front:
   for a in (2,3,4,-4):
    h=compose(f,aa[a])
    if h not in trans:trans[h]=trans[f]+(a,);new.append(h)
  front=new
 assert len(trans)==48
 def normalize(w):
  out=();chunk=()
  for a in tuple(w):
   if abs(a)==1:
    out=red(out+trans[eva(chunk)]+(a,));chunk=()
   else:chunk+=(a,)
  return red(out+trans[eva(chunk)])
 def apply(a,w):
  out=red(y for x in w for y in (mp[a][x-1] if x>0 else inv(mp[a][-x-1])))
  if len(out)>limit:raise OverflowError(('wordcap',a,len(out)))
  return out
 def image(aw,h):
  w=(h+1,)
  for a in reversed(aw):w=apply(a,w)
  return w
 b3=[(2,2),(4,4,4),(2,4,2,4),(3,3),(3,2)*4]
 z=(4,2,-4);b3.append((3,)+z+(-3,)+inv(z))
 rel=[];lengths=[];operatorloops=list(b3);normalizedW=[]
 def addloop(aw):
  assert eva(aw)==ident
  for h in range(9):rel.append(red(image(aw,h)+(-h-1,)))
 for j,w in enumerate(b3):
  addloop(w);print('B3block',j,'maxlength',max(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 for j,w in enumerate(ar['presentation_loops_left_composition']):
  nw=normalize(w);assert eva(nw)==ident
  if nw:operatorloops.append(nw)
  addloop(nw)
  print('Autblock',j,'actionlength',len(nw),'maxword',max(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 for g,w in enumerate(wd['words']):
  aw=normalize(w);assert eva(aw)==tuple(map(tuple,d['generators'][g]))
  normalizedW.append(aw)
  for h in range(9):rel.append(red(image(aw,h)+(g+1,-h-1,-g-1)))
  print('Peifferblock',g,'maxword',max(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 out={'n':3,'endomorphisms':[mp[i] for i in (1,2,3,4,-1,-2,-3,-4)],'iterated_relators':rel,'fixed_relators':[],'aut_operator_relators':operatorloops,'magnus_aut_words_normalized':normalizedW,'proof':'B3 short presentation plus normalized complete Aut relators plus Peiffer; exact inverse endomorphisms included'}
 Path('workspace/agent2_compact_cover_seeds.json').write_text(json.dumps(out))
 print('Saved',len(rel),'relators totalletters',sum(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':generate()
```

```
 out=defaultdict(int)
 for P in Ps:
  for w,c in P.items():out[w]+=int(c)
 return {w:c for w,c in out.items() if c}
def scale(P,c):return {w:int(c)*v for w,v in P.items() if c*v}
def mul(P,Q):
 out=defaultdict(int)
 for u,c in P.items():
  for v,d in Q.items():out[u+v]+=c*d
 return {w:c for w,c in out.items() if c}
def br(P,Q):return add(mul(P,Q),scale(mul(Q,P),-1))
X=[{(i,):1} for i in range(3)]
def lyndon(w):return all(w<w[k:]+w[:k] for k in range(1,len(w)))
LW={d:[w for w in product(range(3),repeat=d) if lyndon(w)] for d in range(1,8)}
LP={}
for d in range(1,8):
 for w in LW[d]:
  if d==1:LP[w]={w:1}
  else:
   k=next(k for k in range(1,d) if w[k:] in LP)
   assert w[:k] in LP
   LP[w]=br(LP[w[:k]],LP[w[k:]])
  assert min(LP[w])==w and LP[w][w]==1

def coords(P,d):
 P=dict(P);out=[]
 for w in LW[d]:
  c=P.get(w,0);out.append(c)
  if c:
   for u,e in LP[w].items():
    v=P.get(u,0)-c*e
    if v:P[u]=v
    else:P.pop(u,None)
 assert not P,P
 return out
def fromcoords(v,d):
 return tuple(add(*(scale(LP[w],int(c)) for w,c in zip(LW[d],v[i*len(LW[d]):(i+1)*len(LW[d])]) if c)) for i in range(3))

def bracket_e(D,e):
 p,q,r=e;out=[]
 for i,Di in enumerate(D):
  val=defaultdict(int)
  if i==p:
   for w,c in add(br(D[q],X[r]),br(X[q],D[r])).items():val[w]+=c
  for w,c in Di.items():
   for k,t in enumerate(w):
    if t==p:
     val[w[:k]+(q,r)+w[k+1:]]-=c
     val[w[:k]+(r,q)+w[k+1:]]+=c
  out.append({w:c for w,c in val.items() if c})
 return tuple(out)
E=[(i,j,k) for i in range(3) for j,k in combinations(range(3),2)]

from collections import Counter
B=matrix.identity(ZZ,9)
for c in range(1,7):
 t=time.time()
 if c>1:
  Ds=[fromcoords(list(v),c) for v in B.rows()]
  rows=[]
  for D in Ds:
   for e in E:
    T=bracket_e(D,e)
    rows.append(sum((coords(P,c+1) for P in T),[]))
  A=matrix(ZZ,rows)
  weights=[tuple(w.count(j)-(j==i) for j in range(3)) for i in range(3) for w in LW[c+1]]
  colgroups=defaultdict(list);rowgroups=defaultdict(list)
  for j,w in enumerate(weights):colgroups[w].append(j)
  for i,row in enumerate(A.rows()):
   nz=row.nonzero_positions()
   if nz:
    wt=weights[nz[0]];assert all(weights[j]==wt for j in nz)
    rowgroups[wt].append(i)
  basis=[];ed=[]
  for wt,rs in rowgroups.items():
   cs=colgroups[wt]
   H=A.matrix_from_rows_and_columns(rs,cs).hermite_form(algorithm='flint',include_zero_rows=False)
   ed+=list(H.elementary_divisors())
   for hr in H.rows():
    row=[0]*A.ncols()
    for j,x in zip(cs,hr):row[j]=x
    basis.append(row)
  B=matrix(ZZ,basis)
 else:ed=[1]*9
 print('degree',c,'rank',B.nrows(),'ambient',B.ncols(),'Smith',Counter(map(int,ed)),'seconds',round(time.time()-t,2),flush=True)
 with open(f'workspace/agent4_johnson_full_degree{c}.pickle','wb') as f:pickle.dump({'basis':B,'lyndon_words':LW[c+1],'smith':ed},f)
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent3_finite_action_lemma.md').write_text(r'''# Finite-action lower bound criterion (audit document)

Let T=F_3<X_1,X_2,X_3>/(words of length >=9), with unit, and let U be
the finite group of algebra automorphisms X_i -> X_i+terms of length >=2.
For g in F_3 (the FREE GROUP of rank three, not the field), substitute
x_i=1+X_i and x_i^{-1}=sum_{k=0}^8(-X_i)^k. Every IA_3 automorphism
therefore induces a well-defined element of U. This is a group
homomorphism (or anti-homomorphism under the opposite composition
convention, which does not affect any lower central subgroup).

Let Q be the image, generated by the nine actual Magnus generators.
An element in gamma_4(Q) has displacement f_i of wordlength >=5.
Two such automorphisms compose by ADDING their displacements:
substituting one displacement into another first contributes length
5-1+5=9 and hence vanishes in T. Consequently gamma_4(Q) is an
abelian group of exponent three, identified with an F_3-subspace of
3 copies of the wordlength 5..8 component of T.

Write L_g for the exactly linear action on this displacement space

To justify the normal-generation assertion, in any group generated by S,

length c in S: induction, since imposing that those length-c commutators
commute with S makes gamma_c central and kills gamma_{c+1}.
Forward invariance under L_g suffices because these are invertible maps
of a finite subspace.

Once V=gamma_c(Q) is contained in this additive displacement space,

This sum is already invariant: each N_h(N_g(V)) is contained in N_h(V),
since V is invariant. Thus no extra group collection or p-power closure
is required in degrees >=4.

If the integral degree-four Johnson map were injective with image rank r,
then gr_4(IA_3) would be free abelian of rank r, and hence a-b<=r.
This needs NO assumption about saturation of the Johnson image.
```

```
Path('workspace/agent3_independent_J4_rank.py').write_text('''"""Exact rational image-rank check by ALL9^4 associative derivation brackets.
No Lyndon bases, image-HNF recursion, or finite-field rank assumptions.
"""
from itertools import combinations,product
from collections import defaultdict
from sage.all import matrix,QQ

def plus(A,B,sgn=1):
 C=A.copy()
 for w,c in B.items():C[w]=C.get(w,0)+sgn*c
 return {w:c for w,c in C.items() if c}
def apply(D,P):
 C=defaultdict(int)
 for w,c in P.items():
  for pos,j in enumerate(w):
   for v,e in D[j].items():C[w[:pos]+v+w[pos+1:]]+=c*e
 return {w:c for w,c in C.items() if c}
def bracket(D,E):return tuple(plus(apply(D,E[i]),apply(E,D[i]),-1) for i in range(3))
G=[];weights=[]
for i in range(3):
 for j,k in combinations(range(3),2):
  D=[{} for _ in range(3)];D[i]={(j,k):1,(k,j):-1};G.append(tuple(D))
  weights.append(tuple(int(a==j)+int(a==k)-int(a==i) for a in range(3)))
levels=[(D,w) for D,w in zip(G,weights)]
for c in range(2,5):
 levels=[(bracket(D,E),tuple(a+b for a,b in zip(w,v))) for D,w in levels for E,v in zip(G,weights)]
 groups=defaultdict(list)
 for D,w in levels:
  if any(D):groups[w].append(D)
 total=0
 for wt,Ds in groups.items():
  keys=sorted(set((i,w) for D in Ds for i,P in enumerate(D) for w in P))
  A=matrix(QQ,[[D[i].get(w,0) for i,w in keys] for D in Ds]);total+=A.rank()
 print('ALL associative simple brackets degree',c,'count',len(levels),'EXACT QQ image rank',total,flush=True)
 assert total=={2:18,3:43,4:120}[c]
''')
```

```
import numpy as np,json,time,sys,os
from itertools import product
from collections import defaultdict
from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix,save_npz,load_npz
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent3_magnus import K,C,magnus,inverse,product as wp
D=8
WORDS=[w for d in range(1,D+1) for w in product(range(3),repeat=d)]
IX={w:j for j,w in enumerate(WORDS)};M=len(WORDS)
def mul(P,Q):
 o=defaultdict(int)
 for w,c in P.items():
  for v,e in Q.items():
   if len(w)+len(v)<=D:o[w+v]+=c*e
 return {w:c%3 for w,c in o.items() if c%3}
def build():
 G=[]
 for i in range(1,4):
  for j in range(1,4):
   if i!=j:G.append(K(3,i,j))
  other=[j for j in range(1,4) if j!=i];G.append(C(3,i,*other))
 ops={}
 for j,pair in enumerate(G):
  for sign,im in zip([1,-1],pair):
   fname=f'workspace/agent1_scalar_S_{j}_{sign}.npz'
   if os.path.exists(fname):ops[sign*(j+1)]=load_npz(fname);continue
   polys=[]
   for w in im:
    P=magnus(w,D);P.pop((),None)
    polys.append({tuple(a-1 for a in v):c%3 for v,c in P.items() if c%3})
   cache={():{():1}};rows=[];cols=[];vals=[];t=time.time()
   for k,w in enumerate(WORDS):
    P=mul(cache[w[:-1]],polys[w[-1]]);cache[w]=P
    for v,c in P.items():rows.append(IX[v]);cols.append(k);vals.append(c)
   S=coo_matrix((np.array(vals,dtype=np.int32),(rows,cols)),shape=(M,M)).tocsr()
   save_npz(fname,S);ops[sign*(j+1)]=S
   print('built scalar',j,sign,'nnz',S.nnz,'seconds',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 return ops
def comm(u,v):return wp(u,v,inverse(u),inverse(v))
def audit():
 ops=build()
 E=np.zeros((M,3),dtype=np.int32);E[:3]=np.eye(3,dtype=np.int32)
 for j in range(1,10):assert np.array_equal((ops[j]@(ops[-j]@E))%3,E)
 source=json.load(open('workspace/agent1_d8_121_seed_provenance.json'))
 target=np.load('workspace/agent3_gamma4_tensor_seedmatrix.npy')
 ix=np.array([IX[w] for w in WORDS if len(w)>=5])
 results=[]
 for row,(k,g) in source.items():
  a=k//81;b=k//9%9;c=k%9
  w=comm(comm(comm((a+1,),(b+1,)),(c+1,)),(-g-1,))
  V=E.copy()
  for s in reversed(w):V=(ops[s]@V)%3
  V=(V-E)%3
  assert not V[:120].any()
  got=V[ix].T.reshape(-1).astype(np.uint8)
  ok=np.array_equal(got,target[int(row)])
  results.append(ok)
  if not ok:
   print('FAIL',row,k,g,'mismatches',np.count_nonzero(got!=target[int(row)]),flush=True)
   np.save('workspace/agent1_d8_failed_actual.npy',got)
   break
 print('literal scalar-matrix audit',sum(results),'of',len(results),'PASS',all(results),flush=True)
 json.dump(results,open('workspace/agent1_d8_121_scalar_checks.json','w'))
if __name__=='__main__':audit()
```

```
p=min(free,key=lambda j:np.count_nonzero(B[:,j]));print('bestfreecoordinate',p,'lambda support',1+np.count_nonzero(B[:,p]),'value',int(r[p]),flush=True)
lam={int(p):1}
for i in np.flatnonzero(B[:,p]):lam[int(P[i])]=int(-int(B[i,p])%3)
assert sum(c*int(w[j]) for j,c in lam.items())%3==r[p]
# coordinate -> output and 0based word.
from itertools import product
WORDS=[v for l in range(5,9) for v in product(range(3),repeat=l)]
terms=[]
for j,c in sorted(lam.items()):terms.append({'output':int(j//9720),'word':WORDS[j%9720],'coefficient':int(c)})
json.dump({'terms':terms,'value_on_W':int(r[p]),'flat_lambda':lam},open('workspace/agent3_W_detector_functional.json','w'))
print(terms)
```

```
import numpy as np,json,time
from scipy.sparse import load_npz,csr_matrix,vstack
D=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_W_detector_functional.json'))
cols=np.array([i*9720+j for i in range(3) for j in range(3159)])
lamcols=[];lamvals=[]
for j,c in D['flat_lambda'].items():
 j=int(j);out,off=divmod(j,9720);assert off<3159;lamcols.append(out*3159+off);lamvals.append(c)
L=csr_matrix((np.array(lamvals,dtype=np.int32),(np.zeros(len(lamcols),dtype=int),lamcols)),shape=(1,9477))
fullix=np.array([i*9801+j for i in range(3) for j in range(81,81+3159)])
N=[load_npz(f'workspace/agent4_tensor_N_{i}.npz')[fullix,:][:,fullix] for i in range(9)]
def clean(A):A=A.tocsr();A.data%=3;A.eliminate_zeros();return A
duals=[clean(L@A) for A in N]
duals+= [clean(d@A) for d in duals[:9] for A in N]
T=vstack(duals,format='csr');A=np.load('workspace/agent3_gamma4_tensor_seedmatrix.npy')[:,cols]
V=(T@A.T)%3
print('dualfunctionals',len(duals),'seedcolumns',len(A),'checkedentries',V.size,'nonzero',np.count_nonzero(V))
assert not V.any()
W=np.load('workspace/agent3_explicit_W_finite_vector.npy')[cols];val=int((L@W)[0]%3);print('lambda W',val);assert val==2
# Independently verify triple nilpotence by degree filtration from every sparse entry.
wordsdeg=np.array([d for d in (5,6,7) for _ in range(3**d)]*3)
for M in N:
 r,c=M.nonzero();assert np.all(wordsdeg[r]>wordsdeg[c])
print('PASS degreefiltration=>alltripleNproducts0')
json.dump({'dual_count':90,'unique_actual_seed_count':5148,'all_dual_seed_pairings_zero':True,'lambda_W':val,'degree_strict_increase':True},open('workspace/agent3_minimal_functional_audit.json','w'))
```
